AUDIT REPORT
Existing structure: Static HTML + Tailwind CSS (CDN) + custom CSS, deployed via GitHub Pages (CNAME → ycbn.org). 8 pages: index, about, programmes, work, stories, team, contact, donate. Resources folder with ~60 images and 6 real PDF reports.

Good things to preserve: Clean layout, real logos/photos, real team data, real event reports (Menstrual Hygiene Day 2026 PDF, IWD 2026 report, CEHURD & Red Cross engagement PDFs), real contact info (info@ycbn.org, +256760315494, P.O Box 117751 Wakiso), Facebook & TikTok accounts.

Problems found:

Fabricated stats — "15 Communities Served, 8 Boreholes, 500 Youth Trained, 12 Schools" counters on index/about/work.
Fabricated news — "New Borehole... 1,500 residents, -60% disease", "500 school girls", "50 youth... 70% internships".
Fabricated impact stories — "1,200 residents", "waterborne diseases 70%", fictional "Sarah" startup story with 40% yields.
Fake testimonial + fake Tax ID on donate page ("Mary, Community Leader", "Tax ID: 8003414616918").
Fake donation form — collects card numbers, fakes success; invented UGX cost claims.
Rickroll YouTube placeholders (dQw4w9WgXcQ) in stories.html videos.
Wrong schema — LocalBusiness type, fake taxID, sameAs include wrong LinkedIn ("tiny-bit-wiser" company) & generic instagram.com; SearchAction to non-existent /search.
Placeholder Google tags — AW-XXXXXXXXXX / G-XXXXXXXXXX published live.
Broken images — stories gallery references non-existent "WhatsApp Image ..." files.
Broken/dead links — Privacy/Terms "#", many "Read More" → "#".
Unsupported claim — "Compliant with NGO Act 2016, WASH sector standards..." in footers.
Missing pages — Volunteer, Partnerships, Transparency/Legal, Policies (Privacy/Terms/Safeguarding/Complaints), program landing pages, 404.
Missing SEO files — no sitemap.xml, robots.txt, per-page titles/meta all identical (duplicate).
No accessiblity — unlabeled inputs (placeholders only), missing aria, no focus-visible handling.
Inconsistent identities — "Safe Water for All" vs "Sustainable WASH for All" in top bar; "© 2024" on team page.
Implementation plan:

Fix honesty issues first (remove all fabricated claims).
New pages: transparency, volunteer, partnerships, privacy, terms, safeguarding, complaints, 404, sitemap, robots, program landing pages (programs/wash, /youth-empowerment, /digital-skills, /innovation, /environment).
Update head/footer/nav with consistent SEO, links, and verified social only (Facebook + TikTok; remove fake LinkedIn/Instagram).
Wire forms honestly (mailto-compose + honeypot) and document manual supply items in a README guide.
Let me start implementing. First, the index.html critical head/SEO/fabrication edits

        // Counter animation
        const counters = document.querySelectorAll('.counter-number');
        const speed = 200;

        const animateCounter = (counter) => {
            const target = parseInt(counter.getAttribute('data-target'));
            const increment = target / speed;
            let current = 0;

            const updateCount = () => {
                current += increment;
                if (current < target) {
                    counter.innerText = Math.ceil(current);
                    requestAnimationFrame(updateCount);
                } else {
                    counter.innerText = target + '+';
                }
            };
            updateCount();
        };

        const observerOptions = { threshold: 0.5 };
        const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
            entries.forEach(entry => {
                if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                    animateCounter(entry.target);
                    observer.unobserve(entry.target);
                }
            });
        }, observerOptions);

        counters.forEach(counter => observer.observe(counter));

        // Close mobile menu on link click
        document.querySelectorAll('#mobileMenu a').forEach(link => {
            link.addEventListener('click', () => {
                mobileMenu.classList.add('hidden');
                const icon = mobileMenuBtn.querySelector('i');
                icon.classList.remove('fa-times');
                icon.classList.add('fa-bars');
            });
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <p>We may update these Terms of Use from time to time. Updates take effect when published here.</p>

<main class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-16">
  <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-2">Safeguarding Policy</h1>
  <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-8">Effective date: [INSERT DATE] &nbsp;|&nbsp; Last updated: [INSERT DATE]</p>
  <div class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-8 content">
    <h2>Our commitment</h2>
    <p>Youth Capacity Building Network (YCBN) works with young people, children and communities. We are committed to protecting everyone who takes part in our programmes and activities, and to creating safe, respectful environments free from harm, abuse and exploitation.</p>
    <p>This policy sets out our approach. It will be supported by detailed procedures, training and reporting arrangements as our programmes develop. [REFERENCES TO THE FULL SAFEGUARDING MANUAL TO BE ADDED WHEN ADOPTED.]</p>
    <h2>Respect and dignity</h2>
    <ul>
      <li>Every participant is treated with respect, fairly and without discrimination.</li>
      <li>We listen to young people and communities and take their views seriously.</li>
      <li>We consider gender, disability, age and vulnerability when designing and delivering activities.</li>
    </ul>
    <h2>Protection from abuse and exploitation</h2>
    <ul>
      <li>We do not tolerate any form of physical, sexual, emotional abuse or exploitation of children, young people or adults in our programmes.</li>
      <li>Staff, volunteers, partners and anyone representing YCBN are expected to carry out background checks, follower agreed conduct standards and receive safeguarding orientation.</li>
      <li>Recruitment and selection of staff and volunteers includes safeguarding requirements.</li>
    </ul>
    <h2>Safe participation</h2>
    <ul>
      <li>Activities involving children and young people are planned in with care to minimise risk and require guardian/parental consent where appropriate.</li>
      <li>Photographs and stories of participants are only used with informed consent, respecting dignity and data protection.</li>
    </ul>
    <h2>Reporting concerns</h2>
    <p>Anyone who has a safeguarding concern about YCBN staff, volunteers, partners or activities, should report it through confidential channels:</p>
    <ul>
      <li>Email: <a href="mailto:info@ycbn.org" class="text-blue-600 underline">info@ycbn.org</a> (marked &ldquo;Confidential - Safeguarding&rdquo;)</li>
      <li>In person: to any YCBN board member, manager or supervisor.</li>
      <li>Through our <a href="complaints.html" class="text-blue-600 underline">Complaints &amp; Feedback process</a>.</li>
    </ul>
    <p>We will take all reports seriously, handle them confidentially and proportionately, and protect reporters from retaliation. [INSERT SAFEGUARDING FOCALPOINT CONTACT WHEN APPOINTED.]</p>
    <h2>Responsible handling of personal information</h2>
    <p>We handle personal data of participants and staff in accordance with our <a href="privacy.html" class="text-blue-600 underline">Privacy Policy</a>, using it only for programme purposes and keeping it secure.</p>
    <h2>Review</h2>
    <p>This policy will be reviewed regularly as YCBN grows, and updated to reflect our actual practices and legal requirements.</p>
  </div>

      <div><h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">1. What is the programme?</h2><p class="text-gray-700">YCBN&rsquo;s WASH programme promotes safe water, sanitation and hygiene in underserved communities and schools in Uganda. Activities are anchored in community priorities and evidence of need.</p></div>
      <div><h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">2. What problem does it address?</h2><p class="text-gray-700">Many communities and schools lack reliable safe water, adequate sanitation facilities and hygiene awareness. Poor WASH conditions contribute to disease, absenteeism and dignity issues, especially for girls and women.</p></div>
      <div><h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">3. Who does it serve?</h2><p class="text-gray-700">Schools (pupils, teachers and families), young people and community members in the areas where YCBN operates.</p></div>

What does YCBN do?</h2><p class="text-gray-700">Planned and emerging activities include: hygiene promotion and handwashing education, menstrual health &amp; hygiene management (MHM) awareness sessions in schools, supporting clean water point maintenance, and WASH-related community engagement and events. [Specific project activities will be listed as they are confirmed and funded.]</p></div>

  $p = Join-Path $root $f
  $lines = Get-Content $p
  # find corrupted top-bar social start line: the line with the top-bar 'flex items-center gap-4'
  $start = -1

$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
function Add-NavLinks($f){
  $p = Join-Path $root $f
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($p)
  $orig = $c
  # Desktop: add Volunteer + Partners after the News link (which references stories.html)
  $c = $c -replace '(?s)(<a href="stories\.html" class="nav-link(?: active)?">[^<]*</a>)', '$1
                    <a href="volunteer.html" class="nav-link">Volunteer</a>
                    <a href="partnerships.html" class="nav-link">Partners</a>
                    <a href="donate.html" class="nav-link">Support</a>'
  # Mobile menu: add Volunteer/Partnerships/Support before Contact link
  $c = $c -replace '(?s)(<a href="contact\.html" class="block py-3 text-\n?[^>]*>[^<]*</a>)', '<a href="volunteer.html" class="block py-3 text-white border-b border-gray-700 font-semibold">Volunteer</a>
                <a href="partnerships.html" class="block py-3 text-white border-b border-gray-700 font-semibold">Partnerships</a>
                <a href="donate.html" class="block py-3 text-white border-b border-gray-700 font-semibold">Support YCBN</a>
                $1'
  [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($p, $c)
  if($c -ne $orig){ Write-Host "$f nav links added" } else { Write-Host "$f nav links NOT matched" }
}

function FixFooter($f){
  $p = Join-Path $root $f
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($p)
  $orig = $c
  $c = $c -replace '(?s)<h4 class="footer-heading">Quick Links</h4>.*?<h4 class="footer-heading">Programmes</h4>', '<h4 class="footer-heading">Quick Links</h4><nav class="flex flex-col"><a href="index.html" class="footer-link">Home</a><a href="about.html" class="footer-link">About YCBN</a><a href="programmes.html" class="footer-link">Our Programmes</a><a href="work.html" class="footer-link">Our Work</a><a href="stories.html" class="footer-link">News &amp; Updates</a><a href="volunteer.html" class="footer-link">Volunteer</a><a href="partnerships.html" class="footer-link">Partnerships</a><a href="transparency.html" class="footer-link">Organization &amp; Legal Info</a><a href="contact.html" class="footer-link">Contact</a></nav></div><div><h4 class="footer-heading">Programmes</h4>'
  $c = $c -replace '(?s)(<h4 class="footer-heading">Programmes</h4>.*?</nav>.*?</div>)(\s*<div>\s*<h4 class="footer-heading">[^<]+</h4>)', '$1<div><h4 class="footer-heading">Policies &amp; Contact</h4><nav class="flex flex-col mb-3"><a href="privacy.html" class="footer-link">Privacy Policy</a><a href="terms.html" class="footer-link">Terms of Use</a><a href="safeguarding.html" class="footer-link">Safeguarding</a><a href="complaints.html" class="footer-link">Complaints &amp; Feedback</a></nav><p class="text-sm text-gray-400">P.O Box 117751, Wakiso, Uganda<br>info@ycbn.org<br>+256 760 315 494</p></div>$2'
  [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($p, $c)
  if($c -ne $orig){ Write-Host "$f footer updated" } else { Write-Host "$f footer NOT matched" }
}

$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
$bad = @()
Get-ChildItem $root -Recurse -Filter *.html -File | ForEach-Object {
  $file = $_
  $dir = $file.DirectoryName
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($file.FullName)
  $m = [regex]::Matches($c, 'href="([^"]+)"')
  foreach($x in $m){
    $href = $x.Groups[1].Value
    if($href -match '^(https?:|mailto:|tel:|#|javascript:|\.\./resources/)'){ continue }
    # skip sitemap-style absolute and empty
    if($href -eq ''){ continue }
    # resolve relative url -> filesystem path
    $full = Join-Path $dir ($href -replace '/',[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar)
    if(-not (Test-Path $full)){ $bad += ($file.FullName.Replace($root,'') + '  ->  ' + $href) }
  }
}

$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
# map of page -> valid ids
$idMap = @{}
Get-ChildItem $root -Recurse -Filter *.html -File | ForEach-Object { $c=[System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($_.FullName); $idMap[$_.Name] = @([regex]::Matches($c,'id="([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)"') | ForEach-Object { $_.Groups[1].Value } | Sort-Object -Unique) }
$bad = @()
Get-ChildItem $root -Recurse -Filter *.html -File | ForEach-Object {
  $file=$_; $dir=$file.DirectoryName; $c=[System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($file.FullName)
  $m=[regex]::Matches($c,'href="([a-zA-Z0-9_./-]+)\.html#([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)"')
  foreach($x in $m){
    $target=$x.Groups[1].Value; $frag=$x.Groups[2].Value
    # resolve target name (may be in programs/ subfolder)
    $rel = $dir.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\\') + '\\' + $target
    $fn = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($target)  # e.g. wash/index.html -> index.html won't have #... but our pattern requires .html
    # The regex captures up to '.html' so target is path/name.html
    $full = Join-Path $dir ($target -replace '/',[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar)
    if(-not (Test-Path $full)){ $bad += ($file.FullName.Replace($root,'') + ' missing file: ' + $target + '#' + $frag) }
    else {
      $tname = Split-Path $full -Leaf
      if($tname -notin $idMap.Keys){ $bad += ('no id lookup for ' + $tname) }
      elseif($frag -notin $idMap[$tname]){ $bad += ($file.FullName.Replace($root,'') + ' -> ' + $target + '#' + $frag + ' (id not found on ' + $tname + ')') }
    }
  }
}

$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
$bad = @()
Get-ChildItem $root -Recurse -Filter *.html -File | ForEach-Object {
  $file=$_; $dir=$file.DirectoryName; $c=[System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($file.FullName)
  $m=[regex]::Matches($c,'href="([^"]+)"')
  foreach($x in $m){
    $href=$x.Groups[1].Value
    if($href -match '^(https?:|mailto:|tel:|#|javascript:)' ){ continue }
    $base = ($href -split '#')[0]
    if($base -eq ''){ continue }
    if($base -match '^(\.\./resources/|\.\./\.\./resources/)'){ continue }
    $full = Join-Path $dir ($base -replace '/',[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar)
    if(-not (Test-Path $full) -and -not (Test-Path ($full + '.html'))){ $bad += ($file.FullName.Replace($root,'') + '  ->  ' + $href) }
  }
}

$pattern = '(?s)<div class="flex gap-3">.*?</div>'
$eval = [System.Text.RegularExpressions.MatchEvaluator]{
  param($m)
  $block = $m.Value
  if($block -notmatch '61579627535321' -and $block -notmatch 'youth\.capacity\.bu'){ return $m.Value }
  $style = 'top'
  if($block -match 'w-10 h-10'){ $style = 'follow' }
  elseif($block -match 'bg-white/10'){ $style = 'footer' }
  $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
  if($style -eq 'top'){ $cls='w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-ycbn-orange transition-colors' }
  elseif($style -eq 'footer'){ $cls='w-9 h-9 rounded bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-ycbn-blue transition-colors text-white' }
  else { $cls='w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-ycbn-gray flex items-center justify-center text-ycbn-blue hover:bg-ycbn-blue hover:text-white transition-colors' }
  [void]$sb.AppendLine('<div class="flex gap-3">')
  foreach($e in $entries){
    $indent = if($style -eq 'follow'){ '                            ' } else { '                        ' }
    $iconSize = if($style -eq 'follow'){ '' } else { ' text-sm' }
    $line = $indent + '<a href="' + $e.Url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="' + $cls + '" aria-label="YCBN on ' + $e.Name + '"><i class="' + $e.Icon + $iconSize + '"></i></a>'
    [void]$sb.AppendLine($line)
  }
  [void]$sb.Append('                    </div>')
  return $sb.ToString()
}

  @{Name='LinkedIn'; Url='https://www.linkedin.com/company/tiny-bit-wiser/'; Icon='fab fa-linkedin-in'},
  @{Name='Instagram'; Url='https://www.instagram.com/youthcapacitybuildingnetwork'; Icon='fab fa-instagram'},
  @{Name='X'; Url='https://x.com/YCBNetwork'; Icon='fab fa-x-twitter'},
  @{Name='YouTube'; Url='https://youtube.com/@youthcapacitybuildingnetwork'; Icon='fab fa-youtube'},
  @{Name='TikTok'; Url='https://www.tiktok.com/@youth.capacity.bu'; Icon='fab fa-tiktok'}
)
function BuildSocial($indent, $pad){
  $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
  foreach($e in $entries){
    [void]$sb.AppendLine($indent + '<a href="' + $e.Url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="w-9 h-9 rounded bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-ycbn-blue transition-colors text-white" aria-label="YCBN on ' + $e.Name + '"><i class="' + $e.Icon + ' text-sm"></i></a>')
  }
  return $sb.ToString().TrimEnd()
}
$socialBlock = '<div class="flex gap-3">' + "`n" + (BuildSocial) + "`n" + '</div>'
# 1) donate/volunteer/partnerships footer first column insert
foreach($f in @('donate.html','volunteer.html','partnerships.html')){
  $p = Join-Path $root $f
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($p)
  $orig = $c
  $m = [regex]::Match($c,'(?s)(<p class="leading-relaxed mb-4">[^<]*</p>)(</div>)')
  if($m){
    $c = $c.Replace($m.Groups[1].Value + $m.Groups[2].Value, $m.Groups[1].Value + $socialBlock + $m.Groups[2].Value)
  }
  # also update donate text mentions
  $c = $c.Replace('follow our work on Facebook and TikTok.','follow our work on Facebook, LinkedIn, Instagram, X, YouTube and TikTok.')
  if($c -ne $orig){ [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($p,$c); Write-Host ($f + ' footer social added') } else { Write-Host ($f + ' NO MATCH') }
}

  $p = Join-Path $root $f
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($p)
  $o = $c
  $c = $c -replace 'cdnjs\.cloudflare\.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6\.4\.0/css/all\.min\.css','cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.7.2/css/all.min.css'
  if($c -ne $o){ [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($p,$c); Write-Host ($f + ': FA 6.4.0 -> 6.7.2') } else { Write-Host ($f + ': NO FA line changed') }
}
# 2) add FA link to pages without it (skip 404)
$add = @('donate.html','volunteer.html','partnerships.html','privacy.html','terms.html','safeguarding.html','complaints.html')
foreach($f in $add){
  $p = Join-Path $root $f
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($p)
  if($c -match 'font-awesome/6\.'){ Write-Host ($f+': already has FA, skip'); continue }
  $o = $c
  $c = $c -replace '(?s)(<script src="https://cdn\.tailwindcss\.com"></script>)', ('$1' + "`n" + $newFA)
  if($c -ne $o){ [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($p,$c); Write-Host ($f + ': FA CDN added') } else { Write-Host ($f + ': NO tailwind line found') }
}

Cline wants to execute this command:
Completed
$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
$cssDir = Join-Path $root 'resources\css'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $cssDir | Out-Null
$sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
$processed = @{}
$files = Get-ChildItem $root -Recurse -Filter *.html -File | Sort-Object Name
foreach($f in $files){
  $c = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($f.FullName)
  $m = [regex]::Match($c, '(?s)<style>\s*(.*?)\s*</style>')
  if($m.Success -and -not $processed.ContainsKey($m.Groups[1].Value.GetHashCode())){
    [void]$sb.AppendLine('/* ==== from ' + $f.FullName.Replace($root,'') + ' ==== */')
    [void]$sb.AppendLine($m.Groups[1].Value.Trim())
    [void]$sb.AppendLine('')
    $processed[$m.Groups[1].Value.GetHashCode()] = $true
  }
}
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $cssDir 'ycbn.css'), $sb.ToString())
Write-Host ('ycbn.css written: ' + (Get-Item (Join-Path $cssDir 'ycbn.css')).Length + ' bytes, ' + $processed.Count + ' unique style blocks')

$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
$navT=[IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root '_chrome\nav.html'))
$footT=[IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root '_chrome\footer.html'))
$waT=[IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root '_chrome\whatsapp.html'))
function Apply-Chrome([string]$file,[string]$prefix,[hashtable]$act,[string]$extraScript){
  $p=Join-Path $root $file
  $L=@(Get-Content $p); $n=$L.Count
  $bi=-1; for($i=0;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '<body'){ $bi=$i; break } }
  if($bi -lt 0){ throw "no body: $file" }
  $si=-1; $se=-1; for($i=0;$i -lt $bi;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '<style>'){ $si=$i; break } }
  if($si -ge 0){ for($i=$si;$i -lt $bi;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</style>'){ $se=$i; break } } }
  $ne=-1; for($i=$bi;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</nav>'){ $ne=$i; break } }
  if($ne -lt 0){ throw "no nav: $file" }
  $fs=-1; for($i=$ne+1;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '<footer'){ $fs=$i; break } }
  if($fs -lt 0){ throw "no footer: $file" }
  $fe=-1; for($i=$fs;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</footer>'){ $fe=$i; break } }
  if($fe -lt 0){ throw "no footer end: $file" }
  $be=-1; for($i=$fe+1;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</body>'){ $be=$i; break } }
  $nav=($navT -replace '\{PREFIX\}',$prefix)
  foreach($k in $act.Keys){ $nav=$nav -replace ('\{'+$k+'\}'),$act[$k] }
  $nav=$nav -replace '\{ACTIVE_[A-Z]+\}',''
  $foot=$footT -replace '\{PREFIX\}',$prefix
  $cssHref = $prefix + 'resources/css/ycbn.css'
  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  for($i=0;$i -lt $bi;$i++){
    if($i -eq $si){ $out.Add('    <link rel="stylesheet" href="'+$cssHref+'">'); $i=$se }
    else { $out.Add($L[$i]) }
  }
  $out.Add('<body class="bg-ycbn-gray antialiased">')
  foreach($l in ($nav -split "`n")){ $out.Add($l) }
  for($i=$ne+1;$i -lt $fs;$i++){ $out.Add($L[$i]) }
  foreach($l in ($foot -split "`n")){ $out.Add($l) }
  foreach($l in ($waT -split "`n")){ $out.Add($l) }
  if($extraScript){ $out.Add($extraScript) }
  for($i=$be;$i -lt $n;$i++){ $out.Add($L[$i]) }
  [IO.File]::WriteAllLines($p,$out)
  Write-Host ('OK  ' + $file)
}

        // Contact form: opens the visitor's email client with a pre-filled message
        const contactForm = document.getElementById('contactForm');
        const formStatus = document.getElementById('formStatus');
        if (contactForm) {
            contactForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const firstName = document.getElementById('firstName').value.trim();
                const lastName = document.getElementById('lastName').value.trim();
                const email = document.getElementById('email').value.trim();
                const phone = (document.getElementById('phone').value || '').trim();
                const subject = document.getElementById('subject').value;
                const message = document.getElementById('message').value.trim();
                if (!firstName || !lastName || !email || !subject || !message) { alert('Please fill in all required fields.'); return; }
                const subjectLine = 'Contact: ' + subject + ' - ' + firstName + ' ' + lastName;
                const body = 'Hello YCBN,\n\nName: ' + firstName + ' ' + lastName + '\nEmail: ' + email + '\n' + (phone ? 'Phone: ' + phone + '\n' : '') + 'Topic: ' + subject + '\n\nMessage:\n' + message + '\n\n---\nSent via the YCBN website contact form.';
                window.location.href = 'mailto:info@ycbn.org?subject=' + encodeURIComponent(subjectLine) + '&body=' + encodeURIComponent(body);
                if (formStatus) { formStatus.classList.remove('hidden'); formStatus.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-info-circle mr-2"></i>Your email application should now open with your message ready to send.'; }
            });
        }
    </script>

$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$root='d:\Emmanuel\ycbn.org-main (1)\ycbn.org-main'
$navT=[IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root '_chrome\nav.html'))
$footT=[IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root '_chrome\footer.html'))
$waT=[IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root '_chrome\whatsapp.html'))
function Apply-Chrome([string]$file,[string]$prefix,[hashtable]$act,[string]$extraScript){
  $p=Join-Path $root $file
  $L=@(Get-Content $p); $n=$L.Count
  $bi=-1; for($i=0;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '<body'){ $bi=$i; break } }
  $si=-1; $se=-1; for($i=0;$i -lt $bi;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '<style>'){ $si=$i; break } }
  if($si -ge 0){ for($i=$si;$i -lt $bi;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</style>'){ $se=$i; break } } }
  $ne=-1; for($i=$bi;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</nav>'){ $ne=$i; break } }
  if($ne -lt 0){ throw "no nav: $file" }
  $fs=-1; for($i=$ne+1;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '<footer'){ $fs=$i; break } }
  if($fs -lt 0){ throw "no footer: $file" }
  $fe=-1; for($i=$fs;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</footer>'){ $fe=$i; break } }
  $be=-1; for($i=$fe+1;$i -lt $n;$i++){ if($L[$i] -match '</body>'){ $be=$i; break } }
  $nav=($navT -replace '\{PREFIX\}',$prefix)
  foreach($k in $act.Keys){ $nav=$nav -replace ('\{'+$k+'\}'),$act[$k] }
  $nav=$nav -replace '\{ACTIVE_[A-Z]+\}',''
  $foot=$footT -replace '\{PREFIX\}',$prefix
  $cssHref = $prefix + 'resources/css/ycbn.css'
  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  for($i=0;$i -lt $bi;$i++){
    if($i -eq $si){ $out.Add('    <link rel="stylesheet" href="'+$cssHref+'">'); $i=$se }
    else { $out.Add($L[$i]) }
  }
  $out.Add('<body class="bg-ycbn-gray antialiased">')
  foreach($l in ($nav -split "`n")){ $out.Add($l) }
  for($i=$ne+1;$i -lt $fs;$i++){ $out.Add($L[$i]) }
  foreach($l in ($foot -split "`n")){ $out.Add($l) }
  foreach($l in ($waT -split "`n")){ $out.Add($l) }
  if($extraScript){ $out.Add($extraScript) }
  for($i=$be;$i -lt $n;$i++){ $out.Add($L[$i]) }
  [IO.File]::WriteAllLines($p,$out)
  Write-Host ('OK  ' + $file)
}
